```
1	import { writeFileSync, copyFileSync, unlinkSync, existsSync, statSync } from 'fs';
2	import { resolve, dirname, basename, extname } from 'path';
3	import { fileURLToPath } from 'url';
4	
5	// ESM compatibility - __dirname is not available in ESM
6	const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
7	const __dirname = dirname(__filename);
8	
9	/**
10	 * Utilities for creating and managing test files.
11	 * Files are created in tests/web-e2e/fixtures/files/ and tracked for cleanup.
12	 *
13	 * Static fixtures (test-image.png, test-document.pdf, test-file.txt)
14	 * are committed to the repo. Generated files use unique names and are
15	 * cleaned up after each test run.
16	 */
17	
18	const FIXTURES_DIR = resolve(__dirname, '../fixtures/files');
19	const createdFiles: string[] = [];
20	
21	interface TestTextFile {
22	  name: string;
23	  content: string;
24	  path: string;
25	}
26	
27	interface TestBinaryFile {
28	  name: string;
29	  path: string;
30	  sizeKb: number;
31	}
32	
33	/**
34	 * Creates a test text file with specified name and content.
35	 * File is tracked for cleanup.
36	 *
37	 * @param name - Optional file name (defaults to test-file-{timestamp}.txt)
38	 * @param content - Optional file content (defaults to test content with timestamp)
39	 * @returns Object with file name, content, and absolute path
40	 */
41	export function createTestTextFile(name?: string, content?: string): TestTextFile {
42	  const timestamp = Date.now();
43	  const fileName = name || `test-file-${timestamp}.txt`;
44	  const fileContent =
45	    content || `Test file created at ${new Date(timestamp).toISOString()}\nThis is test content.`;
46	  const filePath = resolve(FIXTURES_DIR, fileName);
47	
48	  writeFileSync(filePath, fileContent, 'utf-8');
49	  createdFiles.push(filePath);
50	
51	  return {
52	    name: fileName,
53	    content: fileContent,
54	    path: filePath,
55	  };
56	}
57	
58	/**
59	 * Creates a test binary file with specified size.
60	 * File is filled with random bytes and tracked for cleanup.
61	 *
62	 * @param sizeKb - Size of file in kilobytes
63	 * @param name - Optional file name (defaults to test-binary-{timestamp}.bin)
64	 * @returns Object with file name, absolute path, and size
65	 */
66	export function createTestBinaryFile(sizeKb: number, name?: string): TestBinaryFile {
67	  const timestamp = Date.now();
68	  const fileName = name || `test-binary-${timestamp}.bin`;
69	  const filePath = resolve(FIXTURES_DIR, fileName);
70	
71	  // Create buffer with random bytes
72	  const buffer = Buffer.alloc(sizeKb * 1024);
73	  for (let i = 0; i < buffer.length; i++) {
74	    buffer[i] = Math.floor(Math.random() * 256);
75	  }
76	
77	  writeFileSync(filePath, buffer);
78	  createdFiles.push(filePath);
79	
80	  return {
81	    name: fileName,
82	    path: filePath,
83	    sizeKb,
84	  };
85	}
86	
87	/**
88	 * Creates a test image file by copying the static PNG fixture.
89	 * The fixture is a valid 1x1 green PNG (69 bytes).
90	 * File is tracked for cleanup.
91	 *
92	 * @param name - Optional file name (defaults to test-image-{timestamp}.png)
93	 * @returns Object with file name and absolute path
94	 */
95	export function createTestImageFile(name?: string): TestBinaryFile {
96	  const timestamp = Date.now();
97	  const fileName = name || `test-image-${timestamp}.png`;
98	  const filePath = resolve(FIXTURES_DIR, fileName);
99	  const fixturePath = resolve(FIXTURES_DIR, 'test-image.png');
100	
101	  copyFileSync(fixturePath, filePath);
102	  createdFiles.push(filePath);
103	
104	  return {
105	    name: fileName,
106	    path: filePath,
107	    sizeKb: 0,
108	  };
109	}
110	
111	/**
112	 * Removes all test files created during the test session.
113	 * Should be called in afterEach or afterAll hooks.
114	 */
115	export function cleanupTestFiles(): void {
116	  for (const filePath of createdFiles) {
117	    try {
118	      if (existsSync(filePath)) {
119	        unlinkSync(filePath);
120	      }
121	    } catch (error) {
122	      console.warn(`Failed to cleanup test file ${filePath}:`, error);
123	    }
124	  }
125	  createdFiles.length = 0; // Clear the array
126	}
127	
128	/**
129	 * Get absolute path for a file in the test fixtures directory.
130	 *
131	 * @param name - File name
132	 * @returns Absolute path to the file
133	 */
134	export function getTestFilePath(name: string): string {
135	  return resolve(FIXTURES_DIR, name);
136	}
137	
138	/**
139	 * Creates a test media file by copying a committed fixture (e.g. test-video.mp4)
140	 * to a unique name. File is tracked for cleanup.
141	 *
142	 * @param sourceFixture - Name of the committed fixture file (e.g. 'test-video.mp4')
143	 * @param name - Optional destination file name (defaults to test-media-{timestamp}.{ext})
144	 * @returns Object with file name, absolute path, and size in KB
145	 */
146	export function createTestMediaFile(sourceFixture: string, name?: string): TestBinaryFile {
147	  const timestamp = Date.now();
148	  const safeSource = basename(sourceFixture);
149	  const ext = extname(safeSource).replace('.', '') || 'bin';
150	  const fileName = basename(name || `test-media-${timestamp}.${ext}`);
151	  const fixturePath = resolve(FIXTURES_DIR, safeSource);
152	  const filePath = resolve(FIXTURES_DIR, fileName);
153	  const stats = statSync(fixturePath);
154	  copyFileSync(fixturePath, filePath);
155	  createdFiles.push(filePath);
156	  return { name: fileName, path: filePath, sizeKb: Math.ceil(stats.size / 1024) };
157	}
158	
159	/**
160	 * Check if a test file exists.
161	 *
162	 * @param name - File name
163	 * @returns True if file exists
164	 */
165	export function testFileExists(name: string): boolean {
166	  return existsSync(getTestFilePath(name));
167	}
168	
```

{
  "replace_all": false,

  "old_string": "export function createTestImageFile(name?: string): TestBinaryFile {\n  const timestamp = Date.now();\n  const fileName = name || `test-image-${timestamp}.png`;\n  const filePath = resolve(FIXTURES_DIR, fileName);\n  const fixturePath = resolve(FIXTURES_DIR, 'test-image.png');\n\n  copyFileSync(fixturePath, filePath);\n  createdFiles.push(filePath);",

}

1	import type { Page } from '@playwright/test';
2	
3	/**
4	 * Delete the currently-logged-in account by calling the API from within
5	 * the page context.
6	 *
7	 * Uses fetch with credentials to send the refresh-token cookie, obtains
8	 * a fresh access token, then calls DELETE /auth/account.
9	 *
10	 * This helper is designed for afterAll teardown -- it catches ALL errors
11	 * and logs warnings instead of throwing, so test failures are never
12	 * masked by cleanup issues.
13	 */
14	export async function deleteAccountViaPage(page: Page): Promise<void> {
15	  try {
16	    const result = await page.evaluate(async () => {
17	      // Discover API URL from app runtime config or derive from page origin
18	      const apiUrl =
19	        (window as unknown as Record<string, string>).__VITE_API_URL ||
20	        document.querySelector('meta[name="api-url"]')?.getAttribute('content') ||
21	        (window.location.origin.includes('app-staging.')
22	          ? window.location.origin.replace('app-staging.', 'api-staging.')
23	          : window.location.origin.includes('app.')
24	            ? window.location.origin.replace('app.', 'api.')
25	            : 'http://localhost:3000');
```

```
1	name: Release Gate
2	
3	on:
4	  pull_request:
5	    branches: [main]
6	    types: [opened, synchronize, reopened]
7	
8	jobs:
9	  detect-changes:
10	    name: Detect Changes Since Last Release
11	    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
12	    outputs:
13	      desktop: ${{ steps.check.outputs.desktop || steps.release-check.outputs.desktop }}
14	      prev_tag: ${{ steps.check.outputs.prev_tag || steps.release-check.outputs.prev_tag }}
15	      is_release: ${{ steps.release-check.outputs.is_release }}
16	    steps:
17	      - name: Check if release PR
18	        id: release-check
19	        env:
20	          HEAD_REF: ${{ github.head_ref }}
21	        run: |
22	          if [[ "$HEAD_REF" == release-please--* ]]; then
23	            echo "is_release=true" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
24	          else
25	            echo "is_release=false" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
26	            echo "desktop=false" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
27	            echo "prev_tag=" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
28	            echo "Not a release PR — skipping change detection"
29	          fi
30	
31	      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
32	        if: steps.release-check.outputs.is_release == 'true'
33	        with:
34	          fetch-depth: 0
35	
36	      - name: Check for desktop-related changes since last release
37	        if: steps.release-check.outputs.is_release == 'true'
38	        id: check
39	        run: |
40	          # Find the latest non-staging release tag (cipher-box-v* format)
41	          PREV_TAG=$(git tag --list 'cipher-box-v*' --sort=-v:refname \
42	            | grep -v 'staging' \
43	            | head -1)
44	          # Fallback to legacy v* tags if no cipher-box-v* tags exist
45	          if [ -z "$PREV_TAG" ]; then
46	            PREV_TAG=$(git tag --list 'v*' --sort=-v:refname \
47	              | grep -v 'staging' \
48	              | grep -v '@' \
49	              | head -1)
50	          fi
51	
52	          if [ -z "$PREV_TAG" ]; then
53	            echo "No previous release tag found — assuming desktop changed"
54	            echo "desktop=true" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
55	            echo "prev_tag=" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
56	            exit 0
57	          fi
58	
59	          echo "Previous release tag: ${PREV_TAG}"
60	          echo "prev_tag=${PREV_TAG}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
61	          echo "Diffing ${PREV_TAG}..HEAD"
62	
63	          # Desktop-related path patterns (mirrored from desktop-e2e.yml)
64	          DESKTOP_PATTERNS=(
65	            'apps/desktop/src/'
66	            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/'
67	            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/vendor/'
68	            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/capabilities/'
69	            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/resources/'
70	            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/Cargo.toml'
71	            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/Cargo.lock'
72	            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/build.rs'
73	            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/rust-toolchain.toml'
74	            'apps/desktop/index.html'
75	            'apps/desktop/vite.config.'
76	            'apps/desktop/tsconfig'
77	            'packages/crypto/src/'
78	            'packages/crypto/tsconfig'
79	            '.github/workflows/desktop-e2e.yml'
80	          )
81	
82	          CHANGED_FILES=$(git diff --name-only "${PREV_TAG}..HEAD")
83	          DESKTOP_CHANGED=false
84	
85	          for pattern in "${DESKTOP_PATTERNS[@]}"; do
86	            if echo "$CHANGED_FILES" | grep -Fq "$pattern"; then
87	              echo "Desktop change detected matching pattern: ${pattern}"
88	              DESKTOP_CHANGED=true
89	              break
90	            fi
91	          done
92	
93	          echo "desktop=${DESKTOP_CHANGED}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
94	          echo "Desktop changes detected: ${DESKTOP_CHANGED}"
95	
96	  verify-e2e:
97	    name: Verify E2E Passed
98	    needs: detect-changes
99	    if: ${{ always() && !cancelled() }}
100	    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
101	    timeout-minutes: 40
102	    steps:
103	      - name: Fail if detect-changes did not complete (release PR)
104	        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--') && needs.detect-changes.result != 'success'
105	        run: |
106	          echo "::error::detect-changes must succeed before E2E verification."
107	          exit 1
108	
109	      - name: Skip (not a release PR)
110	        if: "!startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--')"
111	        run: echo "Not a release PR — E2E gate not applicable"
112	
113	      - name: Wait for Web E2E and verify it passed
114	        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--')
115	        env:
116	          GH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
117	        run: |
118	          # Get the latest commit on main (the base the release PR targets)
119	          MAIN_SHA=$(gh api repos/${{ github.repository }}/git/ref/heads/main --jq '.object.sha')
120	          echo "Latest main commit: ${MAIN_SHA}"
121	
122	          # Poll for the Web E2E run to complete (it triggers on the same push)
123	          MAX_ATTEMPTS=30
124	          POLL_INTERVAL=30
125	          echo "Waiting for Web E2E run to complete (polling every ${POLL_INTERVAL}s, max ${MAX_ATTEMPTS} attempts)..."
126	
127	          for attempt in $(seq 1 $MAX_ATTEMPTS); do
128	            E2E_CONCLUSION=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
129	              --workflow=e2e.yml --branch=main --limit=10 \
130	              --json headSha,conclusion,status \
131	              --jq "[.[] | select(.headSha == \"${MAIN_SHA}\")][0].conclusion // empty")
132	
133	            if [ -n "$E2E_CONCLUSION" ]; then
134	              echo "Web E2E completed with conclusion: ${E2E_CONCLUSION}"
135	              break
136	            fi
137	
138	            if [ "$attempt" -eq "$MAX_ATTEMPTS" ]; then
139	              echo "::error::Web E2E run did not complete within $(( MAX_ATTEMPTS * POLL_INTERVAL / 60 )) minutes for main (${MAIN_SHA})."
140	              exit 1
141	            fi
142	
143	            echo "Attempt ${attempt}/${MAX_ATTEMPTS}: E2E still running, waiting ${POLL_INTERVAL}s..."
144	            sleep $POLL_INTERVAL
145	          done
146	
147	          if [ "$E2E_CONCLUSION" != "success" ]; then
148	            echo "::error::E2E Tests did not pass on main (conclusion: ${E2E_CONCLUSION})"
149	            exit 1
150	          fi
151	          echo "Web E2E: passed"
152	
153	      - name: Verify Desktop E2E (if desktop changed)
154	        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--') && needs.detect-changes.outputs.desktop == 'true'
155	        env:
156	          GH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
157	          PREV_TAG: ${{ needs.detect-changes.outputs.prev_tag }}
158	        run: |
159	          MAIN_SHA=$(gh api repos/${{ github.repository }}/git/ref/heads/main --jq '.object.sha')
160	          echo "Desktop changes detected since ${PREV_TAG} — verifying Desktop E2E"
161	
162	          # Helper: check if a workflow run actually executed desktop-e2e jobs
163	          # (not skipped due to change detection). Returns 0 if tests ran, 1 if skipped, 2 on API error.
164	          run_executed_tests() {
165	            local run_id=$1
166	            local executed
167	            if ! executed=$(gh api "repos/${{ github.repository }}/actions/runs/${run_id}/jobs" \
168	              --jq '[.jobs[] | select(.name | startswith("Desktop E2E")) | select(.conclusion == "success")] | length'); then
169	              echo "::error::Failed to query jobs for run ${run_id} (API error or rate limit)"
170	              return 2
171	            fi
172	            [ "${executed:-0}" -gt 0 ]
173	          }
174	
175	          # First check if there's a desktop E2E run for HEAD (may still be running)
176	          HEAD_RUN=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
177	            --workflow=desktop-e2e.yml --branch=main --limit=50 \
178	            --json headSha,conclusion,status,databaseId \
179	            --jq "[.[] | select(.headSha == \"${MAIN_SHA}\")][0]")
180	
181	          HEAD_STATUS=$(echo "$HEAD_RUN" | jq -r '.status // empty')
182	
183	          if [ -n "$HEAD_STATUS" ]; then
184	            HEAD_RUN_ID=$(echo "$HEAD_RUN" | jq -r '.databaseId')
185	            echo "Desktop E2E run exists for HEAD (run ${HEAD_RUN_ID}) — polling until complete..."
186	            MAX_ATTEMPTS=40
187	            POLL_INTERVAL=30
188	
189	            for attempt in $(seq 1 $MAX_ATTEMPTS); do
190	              CONCLUSION=$(gh run view "$HEAD_RUN_ID" --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
191	                --json conclusion \
192	                --jq '.conclusion // empty')
193	
194	              if [ -n "$CONCLUSION" ]; then
195	                if [ "$CONCLUSION" != "success" ]; then
196	                  echo "::error::Desktop E2E Tests did not pass on main (conclusion: ${CONCLUSION})"
197	                  exit 1
198	                fi
199	                # Verify the desktop-e2e jobs actually ran (not skipped)
200	                run_executed_tests "$HEAD_RUN_ID" && rc=0 || rc=$?
201	                if [ "$rc" -eq 2 ]; then
202	                  exit 1
203	                elif [ "$rc" -eq 0 ]; then
204	                  echo "Desktop E2E: passed (at HEAD, tests executed)"
205	                  exit 0
206	                else
207	                  echo "Desktop E2E run at HEAD succeeded but tests were skipped (no desktop changes detected by workflow)."
208	                  echo "Falling through to search for a run that actually executed tests..."
209	                  break
210	                fi
211	              fi
212	
213	              if [ "$attempt" -eq "$MAX_ATTEMPTS" ]; then
214	                echo "::error::Desktop E2E run did not complete within $(( MAX_ATTEMPTS * POLL_INTERVAL / 60 )) minutes."
215	                exit 1
216	              fi
217	
218	              echo "Attempt ${attempt}/${MAX_ATTEMPTS}: Desktop E2E still running, waiting ${POLL_INTERVAL}s..."
219	              sleep $POLL_INTERVAL
220	            done
221	          fi
222	
223	          # No desktop E2E run for HEAD (or HEAD run skipped tests) — find the
224	          # most recent run in the release range that actually executed tests
225	          echo "Searching for a Desktop E2E run that executed tests since ${PREV_TAG}..."
226	
227	          if [ -z "$PREV_TAG" ]; then
228	            echo "::error::No previous release tag and no Desktop E2E run for HEAD. Cannot verify desktop changes."
229	            exit 1
230	          fi
231	
232	          # Get commit SHAs in the release range
233	          RANGE_COMMITS=$(gh api "repos/${{ github.repository }}/compare/${PREV_TAG}...${MAIN_SHA}" \
234	            --jq '[.commits[].sha] | join("\n")' 2>/dev/null || echo "")
235	
236	          if [ -z "$RANGE_COMMITS" ]; then
237	            echo "::error::Could not determine commits in range ${PREV_TAG}..${MAIN_SHA}"
238	            exit 1
239	          fi
240	
241	          # Get recent completed desktop E2E runs (newest first) and find the
242	          # most recent one whose commit is within the release range AND whose
243	          # desktop-e2e jobs actually ran (not skipped)
244	          RUNS=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
245	            --workflow=desktop-e2e.yml --branch=main --limit=50 \
246	            --json headSha,conclusion,status,databaseId \
247	            --jq '.[] | select(.status == "completed") | "\(.headSha) \(.conclusion) \(.databaseId)"')
248	
249	          LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA=""
250	          LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION=""
251	
252	          while IFS=" " read -r sha conclusion run_id; do
253	            if [ -z "$sha" ]; then
254	              continue
255	            fi
256	            if echo "$RANGE_COMMITS" | grep -Fq "$sha"; then
257	              if [ "$conclusion" != "success" ]; then
258	                # Found a failed run in range — report it immediately
259	                LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA="$sha"
260	                LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION="$conclusion"
261	                break
262	              fi
263	              # Success run — verify tests actually executed
264	              run_executed_tests "$run_id" && rc=0 || rc=$?
265	              if [ "$rc" -eq 2 ]; then
266	                exit 1
267	              elif [ "$rc" -eq 0 ]; then
268	                LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA="$sha"
269	                LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION="$conclusion"
270	                LATEST_IN_RANGE_RUN_ID="$run_id"
271	                break
272	              else
273	                echo "Skipping run ${run_id} at ${sha} — tests were skipped (no desktop changes detected by workflow)"
274	              fi
275	            fi
276	          done <<EOF
277	          $RUNS
278	          EOF
279	
280	          if [ -z "$LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA" ]; then
281	            echo "::error::No Desktop E2E run found (with tests actually executed) for any commit in ${PREV_TAG}..HEAD. Desktop code changed but was never verified."
282	            exit 1
283	          fi
284	
285	          if [ "$LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION" != "success" ]; then
286	            echo "::error::Most recent Desktop E2E run in range (at ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA}) did not succeed (conclusion: ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION})."
287	            exit 1
288	          fi
289	
290	          echo "Desktop E2E: passed (run ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_RUN_ID} at ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA} with tests executed)"
291	
292	      - name: Skip Desktop E2E (no desktop changes)
293	        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--') && needs.detect-changes.outputs.desktop != 'true'
294	        run: echo "No desktop-related changes since last release — skipping Desktop E2E gate"
295	
296	      - name: Summary
297	        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--')
298	        run: echo "All E2E gates passed — release is safe to merge."
299	
```

> AGENT

Here's the issue:

1. **`e2e.yml`** only triggers on `push` to `main` — not on PRs
2. **`release-gate.yml`** runs on PRs but skips E2E verification for non-release PRs (line 110: "Not a release PR — E2E gate not applicable")

So for feature PRs like #392, E2E tests never run as a required check. The E2E workflow only runs *after* merge to main, and the release gate only verifies E2E *for release-please PRs*.

The question is: should the E2E workflow also trigger on PRs that touch test files? Or should the release gate require E2E verification for all PRs (not just release PRs)?

There are a few options:

1. **Add `pull_request` trigger to `e2e.yml`** with path filters for `tests/web-e2e/**`, `apps/web/**`, `apps/api/**`, `packages/**`
2. **Expand the release gate** to require E2E for PRs that change test/app code (not just release PRs)
3. **Both** — run E2E on PRs that touch relevant paths, and gate on it

Option 1 is the most straightforward — it means E2E tests run on PRs that change test or app code, giving feedback before merge. What's your preference?

> DEVELOPER

the e2e run was correctly kicked off after the pr to main was merged https://github.com/FSM1/cipher-box/actions/runs/23712125460

the issue here is that the release please release gate did not block merging until the web e2e test run was finished.

> AGENT

Right — the release gate only enforces E2E for `release-please--*` branches. So the PR merged to main without waiting for E2E to complete. The E2E run triggered correctly *after* merge, but the gate should have blocked the merge *until* E2E passed.

The fix: the release gate should also require E2E verification for PRs that touch web/API/test code, not just release PRs. Let me check what the current required checks look like on the repo.

Ah, so the "Verify E2E Passed" required check just rubber-stamped it with "Not a release PR — E2E gate not applicable" and passed green. The check was technically "required" but it auto-passed for non-release PRs, making it meaningless as a gate.

The fix: the release gate should actually verify E2E for PRs that touch relevant paths (web, API, tests, packages), not just release-please PRs. Let me update it.

76	
77	      - name: Configure Kubo CORS for browser-based recovery test
78	        run: |
79	          # Allow browser cross-origin requests to Kubo gateway (needed by recovery.html)
80	          docker exec $(docker ps -q --filter ancestor=ipfs/kubo:v0.40.0) ipfs config --json API.HTTPHeaders.Access-Control-Allow-Origin '["*"]'
81	          docker exec $(docker ps -q --filter ancestor=ipfs/kubo:v0.40.0) ipfs config --json API.HTTPHeaders.Access-Control-Allow-Methods '["GET","POST","OPTIONS"]'
82	          docker exec $(docker ps -q --filter ancestor=ipfs/kubo:v0.40.0) ipfs config --json Gateway.HTTPHeaders.Access-Control-Allow-Origin '["*"]'
83	          docker exec $(docker ps -q --filter ancestor=ipfs/kubo:v0.40.0) ipfs config --json Gateway.HTTPHeaders.Access-Control-Allow-Methods '["GET","POST","OPTIONS"]'
84	          # Restart Kubo to apply config (kill + Docker auto-restarts)

89	              echo "Kubo ready with CORS configured"

> AGENT

Got it — the "Verify E2E Passed" check auto-passes for non-release PRs because it skips with "Not a release PR". So PRs with `test:` commits (or any non-release PR) merge freely without E2E verification.

The fix: for non-release PRs that touch web/API/test paths, the release gate should run E2E inline via `workflow_call` and gate on the result.

1. `detect-changes` also checks for web-relevant path changes (for all PRs)
2. New `run-web-e2e` job calls `e2e.yml` via `workflow_call` for non-release PRs with web changes
3. `verify-e2e` checks `run-web-e2e` result for feature PRs instead of auto-passing

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/.github/workflows/release-gate.yml",
  "old_string": "jobs:\n  detect-changes:\n    name: Detect Changes Since Last Release\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    outputs:\n      desktop: ${{ steps.check.outputs.desktop || steps.release-check.outputs.desktop }}\n      prev_tag: ${{ steps.check.outputs.prev_tag || steps.release-check.outputs.prev_tag }}\n      is_release: ${{ steps.release-check.outputs.is_release }}\n    steps:\n      - name: Check if release PR\n        id: release-check\n        env:\n          HEAD_REF: ${{ github.head_ref }}\n        run: |\n          if [[ \"$HEAD_REF\" == release-please--* ]]; then\n            echo \"is_release=true\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\n          else\n            echo \"is_release=false\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\n            echo \"desktop=false\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\n            echo \"prev_tag=\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\n            echo \"Not a release PR — skipping change detection\"\n          fi\n\n      - uses: actions/checkout@v4\n        if: steps.release-check.outputs.is_release == 'true'\n        with:\n          fetch-depth: 0\n\n      - name: Check for desktop-related changes since last release\n        if: steps.release-check.outputs.is_release == 'true'\n        id: check\n        run: |",
  "new_string": "jobs:\n  detect-changes:\n    name: Detect Changes Since Last Release\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    outputs:\n      desktop: ${{ steps.check.outputs.desktop || steps.release-check.outputs.desktop }}\n      prev_tag: ${{ steps.check.outputs.prev_tag || steps.release-check.outputs.prev_tag }}\n      is_release: ${{ steps.release-check.outputs.is_release }}\n      web_changed: ${{ steps.web-check.outputs.changed }}\n    steps:\n      - name: Check if release PR\n        id: release-check\n        env:\n          HEAD_REF: ${{ github.head_ref }}\n        run: |\n          if [[ \"$HEAD_REF\" == release-please--* ]]; then\n            echo \"is_release=true\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\n          else\n            echo \"is_release=false\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\n            echo \"desktop=false\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\n            echo \"prev_tag=\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\n          fi\n\n      - name: Check for web-relevant changes (all PRs)\n        id: web-check\n        env:\n          GH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}\n        run: |\n          CHANGED_FILES=$(gh api \"repos/${{ github.repository }}/pulls/${{ github.event.pull_request.number }}/files\" \\\n            --paginate --jq '.[].filename')\n          WEB_CHANGED=false\n          WEB_PATTERNS=(\"apps/web/\" \"apps/api/\" \"packages/\" \"tests/web-e2e/\")\n          for pattern in \"${WEB_PATTERNS[@]}\"; do\n            if echo \"$CHANGED_FILES\" | grep -Fq \"$pattern\"; then\n              echo \"Web-relevant change detected matching: ${pattern}\"\n              WEB_CHANGED=true\n              break\n            fi\n          done\n          echo \"changed=${WEB_CHANGED}\" >> \"$GITHUB_OUTPUT\"\n          echo \"Web-relevant changes: ${WEB_CHANGED}\"\n\n      - uses: actions/checkout@v4\n        if: steps.release-check.outputs.is_release == 'true'\n        with:\n          fetch-depth: 0\n\n      - name: Check for desktop-related changes since last release\n        if: steps.release-check.outputs.is_release == 'true'\n        id: check\n        run: |"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/.github/workflows/release-gate.yml",
  "old_string": "  verify-e2e:\n    name: Verify E2E Passed\n    needs: detect-changes\n    if: ${{ always() && !cancelled() }}\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    timeout-minutes: 40\n    steps:\n      - name: Fail if detect-changes did not complete (release PR)\n        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--') && needs.detect-changes.result != 'success'\n        run: |\n          echo \"::error::detect-changes must succeed before E2E verification.\"\n          exit 1\n\n      - name: Skip (not a release PR)\n        if: \"!startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--')\"\n        run: echo \"Not a release PR — E2E gate not applicable\"\n\n      - name: Wait for Web E2E and verify it passed\n        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--')",
  "new_string": "  run-web-e2e:\n    name: Run Web E2E (Feature PR)\n    needs: detect-changes\n    if: |\n      !startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--') &&\n      needs.detect-changes.outputs.web_changed == 'true'\n    uses: ./.github/workflows/e2e.yml\n    secrets: inherit\n\n  verify-e2e:\n    name: Verify E2E Passed\n    needs: [detect-changes, run-web-e2e]\n    if: ${{ always() && !cancelled() }}\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    timeout-minutes: 40\n    steps:\n      - name: Fail if detect-changes did not complete (release PR)\n        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--') && needs.detect-changes.result != 'success'\n        run: |\n          echo \"::error::detect-changes must succeed before E2E verification.\"\n          exit 1\n\n      - name: Verify Web E2E (feature PR with web changes)\n        if: |\n          !startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--') &&\n          needs.detect-changes.outputs.web_changed == 'true'\n        run: |\n          if [ \"${{ needs.run-web-e2e.result }}\" != \"success\" ]; then\n            echo \"::error::Web E2E Tests did not pass (result: ${{ needs.run-web-e2e.result }})\"\n            exit 1\n          fi\n          echo \"Web E2E: passed on feature PR\"\n\n      - name: Skip (no web changes)\n        if: |\n          !startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--') &&\n          needs.detect-changes.outputs.web_changed != 'true'\n        run: echo \"No web-relevant changes — E2E not required\"\n\n      - name: Wait for Web E2E and verify it passed\n        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--')"

1	name: Release Gate
2	
3	on:
4	  pull_request:
5	    branches: [main]
6	    types: [opened, synchronize, reopened]
7	
8	jobs:
9	  detect-changes:
10	    name: Detect Changes Since Last Release
11	    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
12	    outputs:
13	      desktop: ${{ steps.check.outputs.desktop || steps.release-check.outputs.desktop }}
14	      prev_tag: ${{ steps.check.outputs.prev_tag || steps.release-check.outputs.prev_tag }}
15	      is_release: ${{ steps.release-check.outputs.is_release }}
16	      web_changed: ${{ steps.web-check.outputs.changed }}
17	    steps:
18	      - name: Check if release PR
19	        id: release-check
20	        env:
21	          HEAD_REF: ${{ github.head_ref }}
22	        run: |
23	          if [[ "$HEAD_REF" == release-please--* ]]; then
24	            echo "is_release=true" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
25	          else
26	            echo "is_release=false" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
27	            echo "desktop=false" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
28	            echo "prev_tag=" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
29	          fi
30	
31	      - name: Check for web-relevant changes (all PRs)
32	        id: web-check
33	        env:
34	          GH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
35	        run: |
36	          CHANGED_FILES=$(gh api "repos/${{ github.repository }}/pulls/${{ github.event.pull_request.number }}/files" \
37	            --paginate --jq '.[].filename')
38	          WEB_CHANGED=false
39	          WEB_PATTERNS=("apps/web/" "apps/api/" "packages/" "tests/web-e2e/")
40	          for pattern in "${WEB_PATTERNS[@]}"; do
41	            if echo "$CHANGED_FILES" | grep -Fq "$pattern"; then
42	              echo "Web-relevant change detected matching: ${pattern}"
43	              WEB_CHANGED=true
44	              break
45	            fi
46	          done
47	          echo "changed=${WEB_CHANGED}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
48	          echo "Web-relevant changes: ${WEB_CHANGED}"
49	
50	      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
51	        if: steps.release-check.outputs.is_release == 'true'
52	        with:
53	          fetch-depth: 0
54	
55	      - name: Check for desktop-related changes since last release
56	        if: steps.release-check.outputs.is_release == 'true'
57	        id: check
58	        run: |
59	          # Find the latest non-staging release tag (cipher-box-v* format)
60	          PREV_TAG=$(git tag --list 'cipher-box-v*' --sort=-v:refname \
61	            | grep -v 'staging' \
62	            | head -1)
63	          # Fallback to legacy v* tags if no cipher-box-v* tags exist
64	          if [ -z "$PREV_TAG" ]; then
65	            PREV_TAG=$(git tag --list 'v*' --sort=-v:refname \
66	              | grep -v 'staging' \
67	              | grep -v '@' \
68	              | head -1)
69	          fi
70	
71	          if [ -z "$PREV_TAG" ]; then
72	            echo "No previous release tag found — assuming desktop changed"
73	            echo "desktop=true" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
74	            echo "prev_tag=" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
75	            exit 0
76	          fi
77	
78	          echo "Previous release tag: ${PREV_TAG}"
79	          echo "prev_tag=${PREV_TAG}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
80	          echo "Diffing ${PREV_TAG}..HEAD"
81	
82	          # Desktop-related path patterns (mirrored from desktop-e2e.yml)
83	          DESKTOP_PATTERNS=(
84	            'apps/desktop/src/'
85	            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/'
86	            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/vendor/'
87	            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/capabilities/'
88	            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/resources/'
89	            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/Cargo.toml'
90	            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/Cargo.lock'
91	            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/build.rs'
92	            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/rust-toolchain.toml'
93	            'apps/desktop/index.html'
94	            'apps/desktop/vite.config.'
95	            'apps/desktop/tsconfig'
96	            'packages/crypto/src/'
97	            'packages/crypto/tsconfig'
98	            '.github/workflows/desktop-e2e.yml'
99	          )
100	
101	          CHANGED_FILES=$(git diff --name-only "${PREV_TAG}..HEAD")
102	          DESKTOP_CHANGED=false
103	
104	          for pattern in "${DESKTOP_PATTERNS[@]}"; do
105	            if echo "$CHANGED_FILES" | grep -Fq "$pattern"; then
106	              echo "Desktop change detected matching pattern: ${pattern}"
107	              DESKTOP_CHANGED=true
108	              break
109	            fi
110	          done
111	
112	          echo "desktop=${DESKTOP_CHANGED}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
113	          echo "Desktop changes detected: ${DESKTOP_CHANGED}"
114	
115	  run-web-e2e:
116	    name: Run Web E2E (Feature PR)
117	    needs: detect-changes
118	    if: |
119	      !startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--') &&
120	      needs.detect-changes.outputs.web_changed == 'true'
121	    uses: ./.github/workflows/e2e.yml
122	    secrets: inherit
123	
124	  verify-e2e:
125	    name: Verify E2E Passed
126	    needs: [detect-changes, run-web-e2e]
127	    if: ${{ always() && !cancelled() }}
128	    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
129	    timeout-minutes: 40
130	    steps:
131	      - name: Fail if detect-changes did not complete (release PR)
132	        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--') && needs.detect-changes.result != 'success'
133	        run: |
134	          echo "::error::detect-changes must succeed before E2E verification."
135	          exit 1
136	
137	      - name: Verify Web E2E (feature PR with web changes)
138	        if: |
139	          !startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--') &&
140	          needs.detect-changes.outputs.web_changed == 'true'
141	        run: |
142	          if [ "${{ needs.run-web-e2e.result }}" != "success" ]; then
143	            echo "::error::Web E2E Tests did not pass (result: ${{ needs.run-web-e2e.result }})"
144	            exit 1
145	          fi
146	          echo "Web E2E: passed on feature PR"
147	
148	      - name: Skip (no web changes)
149	        if: |
150	          !startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--') &&
151	          needs.detect-changes.outputs.web_changed != 'true'
152	        run: echo "No web-relevant changes — E2E not required"
153	
154	      - name: Wait for Web E2E and verify it passed
155	        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--')
156	        env:
157	          GH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
158	        run: |
159	          # Get the latest commit on main (the base the release PR targets)
160	          MAIN_SHA=$(gh api repos/${{ github.repository }}/git/ref/heads/main --jq '.object.sha')
161	          echo "Latest main commit: ${MAIN_SHA}"
162	
163	          # Poll for the Web E2E run to complete (it triggers on the same push)
164	          MAX_ATTEMPTS=30
165	          POLL_INTERVAL=30
166	          echo "Waiting for Web E2E run to complete (polling every ${POLL_INTERVAL}s, max ${MAX_ATTEMPTS} attempts)..."
167	
168	          for attempt in $(seq 1 $MAX_ATTEMPTS); do
169	            E2E_CONCLUSION=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
170	              --workflow=e2e.yml --branch=main --limit=10 \
171	              --json headSha,conclusion,status \
172	              --jq "[.[] | select(.headSha == \"${MAIN_SHA}\")][0].conclusion // empty")
173	
174	            if [ -n "$E2E_CONCLUSION" ]; then
175	              echo "Web E2E completed with conclusion: ${E2E_CONCLUSION}"
176	              break
177	            fi
178	
179	            if [ "$attempt" -eq "$MAX_ATTEMPTS" ]; then
180	              echo "::error::Web E2E run did not complete within $(( MAX_ATTEMPTS * POLL_INTERVAL / 60 )) minutes for main (${MAIN_SHA})."
181	              exit 1
182	            fi
183	
184	            echo "Attempt ${attempt}/${MAX_ATTEMPTS}: E2E still running, waiting ${POLL_INTERVAL}s..."
185	            sleep $POLL_INTERVAL
186	          done
187	
188	          if [ "$E2E_CONCLUSION" != "success" ]; then
189	            echo "::error::E2E Tests did not pass on main (conclusion: ${E2E_CONCLUSION})"
190	            exit 1
191	          fi
192	          echo "Web E2E: passed"
193	
194	      - name: Verify Desktop E2E (if desktop changed)
195	        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--') && needs.detect-changes.outputs.desktop == 'true'
196	        env:
197	          GH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
198	          PREV_TAG: ${{ needs.detect-changes.outputs.prev_tag }}
199	        run: |
200	          MAIN_SHA=$(gh api repos/${{ github.repository }}/git/ref/heads/main --jq '.object.sha')
201	          echo "Desktop changes detected since ${PREV_TAG} — verifying Desktop E2E"
202	
203	          # Helper: check if a workflow run actually executed desktop-e2e jobs
204	          # (not skipped due to change detection). Returns 0 if tests ran, 1 if skipped, 2 on API error.
205	          run_executed_tests() {
206	            local run_id=$1
207	            local executed
208	            if ! executed=$(gh api "repos/${{ github.repository }}/actions/runs/${run_id}/jobs" \
209	              --jq '[.jobs[] | select(.name | startswith("Desktop E2E")) | select(.conclusion == "success")] | length'); then
210	              echo "::error::Failed to query jobs for run ${run_id} (API error or rate limit)"
211	              return 2
212	            fi
213	            [ "${executed:-0}" -gt 0 ]
214	          }
215	
216	          # First check if there's a desktop E2E run for HEAD (may still be running)
217	          HEAD_RUN=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
218	            --workflow=desktop-e2e.yml --branch=main --limit=50 \
219	            --json headSha,conclusion,status,databaseId \
220	            --jq "[.[] | select(.headSha == \"${MAIN_SHA}\")][0]")
221	
222	          HEAD_STATUS=$(echo "$HEAD_RUN" | jq -r '.status // empty')
223	
224	          if [ -n "$HEAD_STATUS" ]; then
225	            HEAD_RUN_ID=$(echo "$HEAD_RUN" | jq -r '.databaseId')
226	            echo "Desktop E2E run exists for HEAD (run ${HEAD_RUN_ID}) — polling until complete..."
227	            MAX_ATTEMPTS=40
228	            POLL_INTERVAL=30
229	
230	            for attempt in $(seq 1 $MAX_ATTEMPTS); do
231	              CONCLUSION=$(gh run view "$HEAD_RUN_ID" --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
232	                --json conclusion \
233	                --jq '.conclusion // empty')
234	
235	              if [ -n "$CONCLUSION" ]; then
236	                if [ "$CONCLUSION" != "success" ]; then
237	                  echo "::error::Desktop E2E Tests did not pass on main (conclusion: ${CONCLUSION})"
238	                  exit 1
239	                fi
240	                # Verify the desktop-e2e jobs actually ran (not skipped)
241	                run_executed_tests "$HEAD_RUN_ID" && rc=0 || rc=$?
242	                if [ "$rc" -eq 2 ]; then
243	                  exit 1
244	                elif [ "$rc" -eq 0 ]; then
245	                  echo "Desktop E2E: passed (at HEAD, tests executed)"
246	                  exit 0
247	                else
248	                  echo "Desktop E2E run at HEAD succeeded but tests were skipped (no desktop changes detected by workflow)."
249	                  echo "Falling through to search for a run that actually executed tests..."
250	                  break
251	                fi
252	              fi
253	
254	              if [ "$attempt" -eq "$MAX_ATTEMPTS" ]; then
255	                echo "::error::Desktop E2E run did not complete within $(( MAX_ATTEMPTS * POLL_INTERVAL / 60 )) minutes."
256	                exit 1
257	              fi
258	
259	              echo "Attempt ${attempt}/${MAX_ATTEMPTS}: Desktop E2E still running, waiting ${POLL_INTERVAL}s..."
260	              sleep $POLL_INTERVAL
261	            done
262	          fi
263	
264	          # No desktop E2E run for HEAD (or HEAD run skipped tests) — find the
265	          # most recent run in the release range that actually executed tests
266	          echo "Searching for a Desktop E2E run that executed tests since ${PREV_TAG}..."
267	
268	          if [ -z "$PREV_TAG" ]; then
269	            echo "::error::No previous release tag and no Desktop E2E run for HEAD. Cannot verify desktop changes."
270	            exit 1
271	          fi
272	
273	          # Get commit SHAs in the release range
274	          RANGE_COMMITS=$(gh api "repos/${{ github.repository }}/compare/${PREV_TAG}...${MAIN_SHA}" \
275	            --jq '[.commits[].sha] | join("\n")' 2>/dev/null || echo "")
276	
277	          if [ -z "$RANGE_COMMITS" ]; then
278	            echo "::error::Could not determine commits in range ${PREV_TAG}..${MAIN_SHA}"
279	            exit 1
280	          fi
281	
282	          # Get recent completed desktop E2E runs (newest first) and find the
283	          # most recent one whose commit is within the release range AND whose
284	          # desktop-e2e jobs actually ran (not skipped)
285	          RUNS=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
286	            --workflow=desktop-e2e.yml --branch=main --limit=50 \
287	            --json headSha,conclusion,status,databaseId \
288	            --jq '.[] | select(.status == "completed") | "\(.headSha) \(.conclusion) \(.databaseId)"')
289	
290	          LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA=""
291	          LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION=""
292	
293	          while IFS=" " read -r sha conclusion run_id; do
294	            if [ -z "$sha" ]; then
295	              continue
296	            fi
297	            if echo "$RANGE_COMMITS" | grep -Fq "$sha"; then
298	              if [ "$conclusion" != "success" ]; then
299	                # Found a failed run in range — report it immediately
300	                LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA="$sha"
301	                LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION="$conclusion"
302	                break
303	              fi
304	              # Success run — verify tests actually executed
305	              run_executed_tests "$run_id" && rc=0 || rc=$?
306	              if [ "$rc" -eq 2 ]; then
307	                exit 1
308	              elif [ "$rc" -eq 0 ]; then
309	                LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA="$sha"
310	                LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION="$conclusion"
311	                LATEST_IN_RANGE_RUN_ID="$run_id"
312	                break
313	              else
314	                echo "Skipping run ${run_id} at ${sha} — tests were skipped (no desktop changes detected by workflow)"
315	              fi
316	            fi
317	          done <<EOF
318	          $RUNS
319	          EOF
320	
321	          if [ -z "$LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA" ]; then
322	            echo "::error::No Desktop E2E run found (with tests actually executed) for any commit in ${PREV_TAG}..HEAD. Desktop code changed but was never verified."
323	            exit 1
324	          fi
325	
326	          if [ "$LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION" != "success" ]; then
327	            echo "::error::Most recent Desktop E2E run in range (at ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA}) did not succeed (conclusion: ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION})."
328	            exit 1
329	          fi
330	
331	          echo "Desktop E2E: passed (run ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_RUN_ID} at ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA} with tests executed)"
332	
333	      - name: Skip Desktop E2E (no desktop changes)
334	        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--') && needs.detect-changes.outputs.desktop != 'true'
335	        run: echo "No desktop-related changes since last release — skipping Desktop E2E gate"
336	
337	      - name: Summary
338	        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--')
339	        run: echo "All E2E gates passed — release is safe to merge."
340	
```

**What changed:**
- `detect-changes` now checks for web-relevant path changes on **all** PRs (not just release PRs)
- New `run-web-e2e` job calls `e2e.yml` via `workflow_call` when a feature PR touches `apps/web/`, `apps/api/`, `packages/`, or `tests/web-e2e/`

> DEVELOPER

this is once again not the issue here - everything works, but since the `test(34):` PR was merged https://github.com/FSM1/cipher-box/commit/78bef4b897570574d0cf2997ec0f4a93ba0408c9 but was not included in the changelog of the next release, the verify-e2e should still have flagged the changes and required the e2e tests to pass before the release please pr was merged. this should have happened automatically

> AGENT

You're right, I was solving the wrong problem. Let me close PR #393 and address the actual issue.

The real problem: when `test(34):` merged to main, the release gate on the release-please PR had already passed from an earlier run. Since `test:` commits don't trigger release-please to update the PR, the gate never re-ran, so stale green checks allowed the release PR to merge without verifying the latest E2E.

The fix: after E2E completes on main, re-trigger the release gate on any open release-please PR so it re-evaluates against the current main.

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/.github/workflows/e2e.yml",
  "old_string": "      - name: Upload Playwright report on failure\n        if: failure()\n        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4\n        with:\n          name: playwright-report\n          path: |\n            tests/web-e2e/playwright-report/\n            tests/web-e2e/test-results/\n          retention-days: 7",
  "new_string": "      - name: Upload Playwright report on failure\n        if: failure()\n        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4\n        with:\n          name: playwright-report\n          path: |\n            tests/web-e2e/playwright-report/\n            tests/web-e2e/test-results/\n          retention-days: 7\n\n  retrigger-release-gate:\n    name: Re-trigger Release Gate\n    needs: web-e2e\n    if: always() && github.event_name == 'push'\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    permissions:\n      actions: write\n    steps:\n      - name: Re-run release gate on open release-please PR\n        env:\n          GH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}\n        run: |\n          # Find any open release-please PR\n          PR_BRANCH=$(gh pr list --repo \"${{ github.repository }}\" \\\n            --search \"head:release-please\" --state open --limit 1 \\\n            --json headRefName --jq '.[0].headRefName // empty')\n\n          if [ -z \"$PR_BRANCH\" ]; then\n            echo \"No open release-please PR — nothing to re-trigger\"\n            exit 0\n          fi\n\n          echo \"Found open release-please PR on branch: ${PR_BRANCH}\"\n\n          # Find the most recent release gate run for this branch\n          RUN_ID=$(gh run list --repo \"${{ github.repository }}\" \\\n            --workflow=release-gate.yml --branch=\"${PR_BRANCH}\" \\\n            --limit 1 --json databaseId,status \\\n            --jq '.[0].databaseId // empty')\n\n          if [ -z \"$RUN_ID\" ]; then\n            echo \"No release gate run found for ${PR_BRANCH} — nothing to re-trigger\"\n            exit 0\n          fi\n\n          echo \"Re-running release gate (run ${RUN_ID}) so it verifies E2E against current main\"\n          gh run rerun \"${RUN_ID}\" --repo \"${{ github.repository }}\""
}

```json
{
  "command": "gh pr create --title \"ci: re-trigger release gate after E2E completes on main\" --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n## Summary\n- When code merges to main (e.g. `test:` commits not in the changelog), the E2E workflow runs but the release gate on any open release-please PR retains stale green checks from before the merge\n- Adds a `retrigger-release-gate` job to `e2e.yml` that runs after E2E completes on main\n- Finds any open release-please PR and re-runs its release gate workflow, forcing it to re-evaluate against the current main state\n\n## Root cause\nThe release gate only triggers on `pull_request: [opened, synchronize, reopened]`. When `test:` commits land on main, release-please doesn't update its PR (since `test:` isn't in the changelog), so no `synchronize` event fires, and the release gate never re-runs. The stale green check allows the release PR to merge without verifying the latest E2E.\n\n## How the fix works\n1. E2E workflow completes on main (push trigger)\n2. New `retrigger-release-gate` job finds any open `release-please--*` PR\n3. Uses `gh run rerun` to re-run the release gate for that PR\n4. Release gate re-evaluates with `MAIN_SHA` pointing to the latest main commit\n5. If E2E failed, the release gate blocks the release PR\n\n## Test plan\n- [ ] Merge a `test:` commit to main → verify release gate re-runs on open release-please PR\n- [ ] E2E passes on main → release gate should re-run and pass\n- [ ] E2E fails on main → release gate should re-run and fail, blocking the release PR\n- [ ] No open release-please PR → retrigger job exits cleanly with \"nothing to re-trigger\"\n\n🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)\nEOF\n)\"",
  "description": "Create PR"
}
```

PR created: https://github.com/FSM1/cipher-box/pull/394

**The fix:** After E2E completes on any push to main, a new `retrigger-release-gate` job checks for open release-please PRs and re-runs their release gate workflow via `gh run rerun`. This forces the gate to re-evaluate `MAIN_SHA` against the latest main, catching cases where `test:` (or other non-changelog) commits land after the gate last ran.

{"comments":[{"author":"copilot-pull-request-reviewer","body":"This selects the most recent release-gate run but doesn't use the returned `status`. If the latest run for the PR branch is `queued`/`in_progress`, `gh run rerun` will fail and could make the main-branch E2E workflow red for an unrelated reason. Filter for `status == \"completed\"` (or wait/poll until completed) before attempting the rerun, otherwise exit 0 with a clear message.\n```suggestion\n          # Find the most recent *completed* release gate run for this branch\n          RUN_ID=$(gh run list --repo \"${{ github.repository }}\" \\\n            --workflow=release-gate.yml --branch=\"${PR_BRANCH}\" \\\n            --limit 20 --json databaseId,status \\\n            --jq 'map(select(.status == \"completed\")) | .[0].databaseId // empty')\n\n          if [ -z \"$RUN_ID\" ]; then\n            echo \"No completed release gate run found for ${PR_BRANCH} — nothing to re-trigger\"\n```","id":"PRRC_kwDOQ6DXvM6zMa7M","line":214,"path":".github/workflows/e2e.yml"}],"threadId":"PRRT_kwDOQ6DXvM53iB1j"}
{"comments":[{"author":"copilot-pull-request-reviewer","body":"`permissions` here overrides the default `GITHUB_TOKEN` permissions for the job, but only `actions: write` is granted. The `gh pr list` call typically requires `pull-requests: read` (and in some orgs also `contents: read`), so this step can fail with a 403 and never re-trigger the gate. Expand the job permissions to include at least `pull-requests: read` while keeping `actions: write` for `gh run rerun`.","id":"PRRC_kwDOQ6DXvM6zMa7X","line":193,"path":".github/workflows/e2e.yml"}],"threadId":"PRRT_kwDOQ6DXvM53iB1s"}

> DEVELOPER

so currently, the web e2e always runs on merges to main while desktop-e2e runs on changes to the desktop app.

would it make sense to gate the web-e2e to only run when the web (or any of its dependencies) is updated?

> AGENT

Yes, that would save CI minutes. But there's a tradeoff: since packages like `crypto`, `core`, `sdk`, and `api-client` are shared dependencies of the web app, the path filter list needs to be comprehensive enough to not miss breakage from upstream changes.

Let me check what paths could affect web E2E.

> AGENT

Makes sense. The desktop-e2e pattern is clean — `dorny/paths-filter` for change detection, then skip the expensive job if nothing relevant changed. Here's the path list that would cover web E2E:

| Path | Reason |
|------|--------|
| `apps/web/**` | Web app itself |
| `apps/api/**` | API (backend for all E2E tests) |
| `packages/crypto/**` | Crypto lib used by web + API |
| `packages/core/**` | Core lib used by web |
| `packages/api-client/**` | Generated API client |
| `packages/sdk/**` | SDK (used by E2E tests directly) |
| `packages/sdk-core/**` | SDK core |
| `tests/web-e2e/**` | The tests themselves |
| `.github/workflows/e2e.yml` | Workflow changes |

One thing to consider: the `retrigger-release-gate` job should still run even when E2E is skipped (since it needs to re-trigger the gate regardless). But that's fine — it only needs the change detection output, not the E2E result.

Want me to implement this using the same `dorny/paths-filter` + conditional pattern as desktop-e2e?

> DEVELOPER

Yes I think this is a great pattern to follow. while we are at it, would it possibly make sense to use once common `detect changes` job driving whether the `web-e2e` and `desktop-e2e` are needed

> AGENT

Good idea. Right now change detection is duplicated — desktop-e2e has its own `dorny/paths-filter`, and we'd be adding another to e2e.yml. A single orchestrator is cleaner.

The approach:

1. **New `ci-e2e.yml`** — triggers on push to main, runs one `detect-changes` job with both web and desktop patterns, conditionally calls each E2E workflow via `workflow_call`, and owns the `retrigger-release-gate` job
2. **`e2e.yml` and `desktop-e2e.yml`** — remove `push` trigger, keep `workflow_call` + `workflow_dispatch` (so manual runs and release gate calls still work)
3. **Release gate** — update polling from `--workflow=e2e.yml` to `--workflow=ci-e2e.yml`

One thing to note: `dorny/paths-filter` uses `github.event.before` which is only available on `push` events — but since the orchestrator is the one triggered by push, that context flows through correctly.

Let me check the desktop-e2e workflow fully to make sure I capture all its patterns and the conditional logic.

> TOOL

}
```

1	name: Desktop E2E Tests
2	
3	on:
4	  push:
5	    branches: [main]
6	  workflow_dispatch:
7	  workflow_call:
8	    inputs:
9	      ref:
10	        description: 'Git ref to checkout (tag, branch, or SHA)'
11	        required: false
12	        type: string
13	
14	jobs:
15	  changes:
16	    name: Detect Changes
17	    # Only run change detection for push events (skip for workflow_dispatch and workflow_call)
18	    if: github.event_name == 'push'
19	    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
20	    outputs:
21	      desktop: ${{ steps.filter.outputs.desktop }}
22	    steps:
23	      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
24	      - uses: dorny/paths-filter@v3
25	        id: filter
26	        with:
27	          base: ${{ github.event.before }}
28	          filters: |
29	            desktop:
30	              - 'apps/desktop/src/**'
31	              - 'apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/**'
32	              - 'apps/desktop/src-tauri/vendor/**'
33	              - 'apps/desktop/src-tauri/capabilities/**'
34	              - 'apps/desktop/src-tauri/resources/**'
35	              - 'apps/desktop/src-tauri/Cargo.toml'
36	              - 'apps/desktop/src-tauri/build.rs'
37	              - 'apps/desktop/src-tauri/rust-toolchain.toml'
38	              - 'apps/desktop/index.html'
39	              - 'apps/desktop/vite.config.*'
40	              - 'apps/desktop/tsconfig*'
41	              - 'crates/**'
42	              - 'Cargo.toml'
43	              - 'Cargo.lock'
44	              - 'tests/vectors/**'
45	              - 'packages/crypto/src/**'
46	              - 'packages/crypto/tsconfig*'
47	              - '.github/workflows/desktop-e2e.yml'
48	
49	  desktop-e2e:
50	    name: Desktop E2E (${{ matrix.platform }})
51	    needs: [changes]
52	    if: >-
53	      always() && !cancelled() &&
54	      (
55	        github.event_name != 'push' ||
56	        needs.changes.outputs.desktop == 'true'
57	      )
58	    runs-on: ${{ matrix.os }}
59	    timeout-minutes: 45
60	    strategy:
61	      fail-fast: false
62	      matrix:
63	        include:
64	          - platform: macos
65	            os: macos-latest
66	            fuse-feature: fuse
67	            binary-name: cipherbox-desktop
68	          - platform: windows
69	            os: windows-latest
70	            fuse-feature: winfsp
71	            binary-name: cipherbox-desktop.exe
72	          - platform: linux
73	            os: ubuntu-22.04
74	            fuse-feature: fuse
75	            binary-name: cipherbox-desktop
76	
77	    steps:
78	      - name: Checkout
79	        uses: actions/checkout@v4
80	        with:
81	          ref: ${{ inputs.ref || github.sha }}
82	
83	      # --- Install FUSE driver (needed for both build and test) ---
84	
85	      - name: Install FUSE-T (macOS)
86	        if: runner.os == 'macOS'
87	        run: |
88	          brew install --cask fuse-t
89	          sudo mkdir -p /usr/local/lib/pkgconfig
90	          FUSE_T_PC="/Library/Application Support/fuse-t/pkgconfig/fuse-t.pc"
91	          if [ ! -f "$FUSE_T_PC" ]; then
92	            FUSE_T_PC=$(find /Library /usr/local /opt/homebrew -name "fuse-t.pc" 2>/dev/null | head -1)
93	          fi
94	          if [ -z "$FUSE_T_PC" ] || [ ! -f "$FUSE_T_PC" ]; then
95	            echo "::error::fuse-t.pc not found after FUSE-T installation"
96	            exit 1
97	          fi
98	          sudo cp "$FUSE_T_PC" /usr/local/lib/pkgconfig/fuse.pc
99	          sudo sed -i '' 's/^Version:.*/Version: 2.9.9/' /usr/local/lib/pkgconfig/fuse.pc
100	
101	      - name: Install system dependencies (Linux)
102	        if: runner.os == 'Linux'
103	        run: |
104	          sudo apt-get update
105	          sudo apt-get install -y \
106	            libwebkit2gtk-4.1-dev \
107	            libayatana-appindicator3-dev \
108	            librsvg2-dev \
109	            libssl-dev \
110	            libxdo-dev \
111	            libfuse3-dev \
112	            fuse3 \
113	            pkg-config \
114	            build-essential
115	          sudo modprobe fuse || true
116	
117	      - name: Install WinFsp (Windows)
118	        if: runner.os == 'Windows'
119	        shell: powershell
120	        run: |
121	          $url = "https://github.com/winfsp/winfsp/releases/download/v2.1/winfsp-2.1.25156.msi"
122	          $out = "winfsp.msi"
123	          $expectedHash = "073A70E00F77423E34BED98B86E600DEF93393BA5822204FAC57A29324DB9F7A"
124	          Invoke-WebRequest -Uri $url -OutFile $out
125	          $actualHash = (Get-FileHash $out -Algorithm SHA256).Hash
126	          if ($actualHash -ne $expectedHash) { throw "WinFsp MSI hash mismatch: expected $expectedHash, got $actualHash" }
127	          Start-Process msiexec.exe -ArgumentList "/i","$out","/qn","INSTALLLEVEL=1000" -Wait -NoNewWindow
128	          # Verify WinFsp installed and add bin to PATH for DLL discovery
129	          $winfspDir = (Get-ItemProperty -Path "HKLM:\SOFTWARE\WOW6432Node\WinFsp" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue).InstallDir
130	          if ($winfspDir) {
131	            Write-Host "WinFsp installed at: $winfspDir"
132	            $binDir = Join-Path $winfspDir "bin"
133	            Write-Host "Adding to PATH: $binDir"
134	            echo "$binDir" | Out-File -FilePath $env:GITHUB_PATH -Encoding utf8 -Append
135	          } else {
136	            Write-Host "WARNING: WinFsp registry key not found after install"
137	          }
138	
139	      # --- Setup Node.js + pnpm (needed for frontend build before cargo) ---
140	
141	      - uses: pnpm/action-setup@v4
142	        with:
143	          version: 10
144	
145	      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
146	        with:
147	          node-version: '22'
148	          cache: 'pnpm'
149	
150	      - name: Install dependencies
151	        run: pnpm install --frozen-lockfile
152	
153	      # --- Build desktop frontend (Tauri embeds from frontendDist) ---
154	
155	      - name: Build desktop frontend
156	        run: |
157	          pnpm --filter @cipherbox/crypto build
158	          pnpm --filter @cipherbox/core build
159	          pnpm --filter @cipherbox/api-client build
160	          pnpm --filter @cipherbox/sdk-core build
161	          pnpm --filter @cipherbox/sdk build
162	          cd apps/desktop
163	          pnpm vite build
164	        env:
165	          VITE_API_URL: http://localhost:3000
166	          VITE_TEST_LOGIN_SECRET=[REDACTED]
167	
168	      # --- Build debug binary ---
169	
170	      - name: Install Rust toolchain
171	        run: rustup default stable
172	
173	      - uses: actions/cache@v4
174	        with:
175	          path: |
176	            ~/.cargo/registry
177	            ~/.cargo/git
178	            target
179	          key: ${{ matrix.platform }}-cargo-${{ hashFiles('Cargo.lock') }}
180	          restore-keys: ${{ matrix.platform }}-cargo-
181	
182	      - name: Create WinFsp MSI placeholder for resource glob (Windows)
183	        if: runner.os == 'Windows'
184	        shell: powershell
185	        run: New-Item -ItemType File -Force -Path "apps/desktop/src-tauri/resources/winfsp-placeholder.msi"
186	
187	      - name: Build debug binary
188	        run: cargo build -p cipherbox-desktop --no-default-features --features ${{ matrix.fuse-feature }}
189	        env:
190	          PKG_CONFIG_PATH: /opt/homebrew/lib/pkgconfig:/usr/local/lib/pkgconfig
191	
192	      - name: Fix rpath for FUSE-T (macOS)
193	        if: runner.os == 'macOS'
194	        run: install_name_tool -add_rpath /usr/local/lib target/debug/${{ matrix.binary-name }} || true
195	
196	      # --- Install backend services ---
197	
198	      - name: Setup PostgreSQL
199	        uses: ikalnytskyi/action-setup-postgres@v8
200	        with:
201	          username: postgres

275	          # Memurai installs as a Windows service and starts automatically
276	          $svc = Get-Service -Name "Memurai" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
277	          if ($svc -and $svc.Status -eq "Running") {
278	            Write-Host "Memurai service is running"
279	          } else {
280	            # Fallback: start service manually
281	            Start-Service -Name "Memurai" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
282	            Start-Sleep -Seconds 3
283	          }
284	          # Verify Redis is responding
285	          $env:PATH = [System.Environment]::GetEnvironmentVariable("PATH", "Machine") + ";" + $env:PATH
286	          $ready = $false
287	          for ($i = 0; $i -lt 10; $i++) {
288	            try {
289	              $result = & redis-cli ping 2>$null
290	              if ($result -eq "PONG") { $ready = $true; break }
291	            } catch {}
292	            Start-Sleep -Seconds 1
293	          }
294	          if ($ready) { Write-Host "Redis (Memurai) ready" } else { Write-Host "WARNING: Redis may not be ready" }

57	    steps:
58	      - name: Checkout code
59	        uses: actions/checkout@v4
60	        with:
61	          ref: ${{ inputs.ref || github.sha }}
62	
63	      - name: Setup pnpm
64	        uses: pnpm/action-setup@v4
65	        with:
66	          version: 10
67	
68	      - name: Setup Node.js
69	        uses: actions/setup-node@v4
70	        with:
71	          node-version: '22'
72	          cache: 'pnpm'
73	
74	      - name: Install dependencies
75	        run: pnpm install --frozen-lockfile
76	
77	      - name: Configure Kubo CORS for browser-based recovery test
78	        run: |
79	          # Allow browser cross-origin requests to Kubo gateway (needed by recovery.html)
80	          docker exec $(docker ps -q --filter ancestor=ipfs/kubo:v0.40.0) ipfs config --json API.HTTPHeaders.Access-Control-Allow-Origin '["*"]'
81	          docker exec $(docker ps -q --filter ancestor=ipfs/kubo:v0.40.0) ipfs config --json API.HTTPHeaders.Access-Control-Allow-Methods '["GET","POST","OPTIONS"]'
82	          docker exec $(docker ps -q --filter ancestor=ipfs/kubo:v0.40.0) ipfs config --json Gateway.HTTPHeaders.Access-Control-Allow-Origin '["*"]'
83	          docker exec $(docker ps -q --filter ancestor=ipfs/kubo:v0.40.0) ipfs config --json Gateway.HTTPHeaders.Access-Control-Allow-Methods '["GET","POST","OPTIONS"]'
84	          # Restart Kubo to apply config (kill + Docker auto-restarts)
85	          docker restart $(docker ps -q --filter ancestor=ipfs/kubo:v0.40.0)
86	          # Wait for Kubo to be healthy again
87	          for i in $(seq 1 30); do
88	            if docker exec $(docker ps -q --filter ancestor=ipfs/kubo:v0.40.0) ipfs id > /dev/null 2>&1; then
89	              echo "Kubo ready with CORS configured"
90	              break
91	            fi
92	            sleep 2
93	          done
94	
95	      - name: Install Playwright browsers

97	
98	      - name: Install mock-ipns-routing dependencies

101	          npm install
102	          npm run build

138	          echo "IDENTITY_JWT_PRIVATE_KEY=${{ secrets.IDENTITY_JWT_PRIVATE_KEY }}" >> apps/api/.env
139	
140	      - name: Run Web E2E tests

142	        env:
143	          CI: true
144	          # Database configuration
145	          DB_HOST: localhost

147	          DB_USERNAME: postgres

149	          DB_DATABASE: cipherbox_test

160	          # Redis for BullMQ job scheduling

164	          VITE_WEB3AUTH_CLIENT_ID: ${{ vars.VITE_WEB3AUTH_CLIENT_ID }}

182	
183	  retrigger-release-gate:
184	    name: Re-trigger Release Gate
185	    needs: web-e2e
186	    if: always() && github.event_name == 'push'
187	    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
188	    permissions:
189	      actions: write
190	      pull-requests: read
191	    steps:
192	      - name: Re-run release gate on open release-please PR
193	        env:
194	          GH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
195	        run: |
196	          # Find any open release-please PR
197	          PR_BRANCH=$(gh pr list --repo "${{ github.repository }}" \
198	            --search "head:release-please" --state open --limit 1 \
199	            --json headRefName --jq '.[0].headRefName // empty')
200	
201	          if [ -z "$PR_BRANCH" ]; then
202	            echo "No open release-please PR — nothing to re-trigger"
203	            exit 0
204	          fi
205	
206	          echo "Found open release-please PR on branch: ${PR_BRANCH}"
207	
208	          # Find the most recent completed release gate run for this branch
209	          RUN_ID=$(gh run list --repo "${{ github.repository }}" \
210	            --workflow=release-gate.yml --branch="${PR_BRANCH}" \
211	            --limit 20 --json databaseId,status \
212	            --jq 'map(select(.status == "completed")) | .[0].databaseId // empty')
213	
214	          if [ -z "$RUN_ID" ]; then
215	            echo "No completed release gate run found for ${PR_BRANCH} — nothing to re-trigger"
216	            exit 0
217	          fi
218	
219	          echo "Re-running release gate (run ${RUN_ID}) so it verifies E2E against current main"
220	          gh run rerun "${RUN_ID}" --repo "${{ github.repository }}"
221

{
  "file_path": "/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/.github/workflows/ci-e2e.yml",
  "content": "name: CI E2E Tests\n\non:\n  push:\n    branches: [main]\n  workflow_dispatch:\n\njobs:\n  detect-changes:\n    name: Detect Changes\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    outputs:\n      web: ${{ steps.filter.outputs.web }}\n      desktop: ${{ steps.filter.outputs.desktop }}\n    steps:\n      - uses: actions/checkout@v4\n\n      - uses: dorny/paths-filter@v3\n        id: filter\n        with:\n          base: ${{ github.event.before || 'HEAD~1' }}\n          filters: |\n            web:\n              - 'apps/web/**'\n              - 'apps/api/**'\n              - 'packages/**'\n              - 'tests/web-e2e/**'\n              - 'tools/mock-ipns-routing/**'\n              - '.github/workflows/e2e.yml'\n              - '.github/workflows/ci-e2e.yml'\n            desktop:\n              - 'apps/desktop/src/**'\n              - 'apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/**'\n              - 'apps/desktop/src-tauri/vendor/**'\n              - 'apps/desktop/src-tauri/capabilities/**'\n              - 'apps/desktop/src-tauri/resources/**'\n              - 'apps/desktop/src-tauri/Cargo.toml'\n              - 'apps/desktop/src-tauri/build.rs'\n              - 'apps/desktop/src-tauri/rust-toolchain.toml'\n              - 'apps/desktop/index.html'\n              - 'apps/desktop/vite.config.*'\n              - 'apps/desktop/tsconfig*'\n              - 'crates/**'\n              - 'Cargo.toml'\n              - 'Cargo.lock'\n              - 'tests/vectors/**'\n              - 'packages/crypto/src/**'\n              - 'packages/crypto/tsconfig*'\n              - '.github/workflows/desktop-e2e.yml'\n              - '.github/workflows/ci-e2e.yml'\n\n      - name: Summary\n        run: |\n          echo \"Web changes: ${{ steps.filter.outputs.web }}\"\n          echo \"Desktop changes: ${{ steps.filter.outputs.desktop }}\"\n\n  web-e2e:\n    name: Web E2E\n    needs: detect-changes\n    if: needs.detect-changes.outputs.web == 'true' || github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch'\n    uses: ./.github/workflows/e2e.yml\n    secrets: inherit\n\n  desktop-e2e:\n    name: Desktop E2E\n    needs: detect-changes\n    if: needs.detect-changes.outputs.desktop == 'true' || github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch'\n    uses: ./.github/workflows/desktop-e2e.yml\n    secrets: inherit\n\n  retrigger-release-gate:\n    name: Re-trigger Release Gate\n    needs: [detect-changes, web-e2e, desktop-e2e]\n    if: always() && github.event_name == 'push'\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    permissions:\n      actions: write\n      pull-requests: read\n    steps:\n      - name: Re-run release gate on open release-please PR\n        env:\n          GH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}\n        run: |\n          # Find any open release-please PR\n          PR_BRANCH=$(gh pr list --repo \"${{ github.repository }}\" \\\n            --search \"head:release-please\" --state open --limit 1 \\\n            --json headRefName --jq '.[0].headRefName // empty')\n\n          if [ -z \"$PR_BRANCH\" ]; then\n            echo \"No open release-please PR — nothing to re-trigger\"\n            exit 0\n          fi\n\n          echo \"Found open release-please PR on branch: ${PR_BRANCH}\"\n\n          # Find the most recent completed release gate run for this branch\n          RUN_ID=$(gh run list --repo \"${{ github.repository }}\" \\\n            --workflow=release-gate.yml --branch=\"${PR_BRANCH}\" \\\n            --limit 20 --json databaseId,status \\\n            --jq 'map(select(.status == \"completed\")) | .[0].databaseId // empty')\n\n          if [ -z \"$RUN_ID\" ]; then\n            echo \"No completed release gate run found for ${PR_BRANCH} — nothing to re-trigger\"\n            exit 0\n          fi\n\n          echo \"Re-running release gate (run ${RUN_ID}) so it verifies E2E against current main\"\n          gh run rerun \"${RUN_ID}\" --repo \"${{ github.repository }}\"\n"
}

286	            fi
287	            if echo "$RANGE_COMMITS" | grep -Fq "$sha"; then
288	              if [ "$conclusion" != "success" ]; then
289	                # Found a failed run in range — report it immediately
290	                LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA="$sha"
291	                LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION="$conclusion"
292	                break
293	              fi
294	              # Success run — verify tests actually executed
295	              run_executed_tests "$run_id" && rc=0 || rc=$?
296	              if [ "$rc" -eq 2 ]; then
297	                exit 1
298	              elif [ "$rc" -eq 0 ]; then
299	                LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA="$sha"
300	                LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION="$conclusion"
301	                LATEST_IN_RANGE_RUN_ID="$run_id"
302	                break
303	              else
304	                echo "Skipping run ${run_id} at ${sha} — tests were skipped (no desktop changes detected by workflow)"
305	              fi
306	            fi
307	          done <<EOF
308	          $RUNS
309	          EOF
310	
311	          if [ -z "$LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA" ]; then
312	            echo "::error::No Desktop E2E run found (with tests actually executed) for any commit in ${PREV_TAG}..HEAD. Desktop code changed but was never verified."
313	            exit 1
314	          fi
315	
316	          if [ "$LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION" != "success" ]; then
317	            echo "::error::Most recent Desktop E2E run in range (at ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA}) did not succeed (conclusion: ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION})."
318	            exit 1
319	          fi
320	
321	          echo "Desktop E2E: passed (run ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_RUN_ID} at ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA} with tests executed)"
322	
323	      - name: Skip Desktop E2E (no desktop changes)
324	        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--') && needs.detect-changes.outputs.desktop != 'true'
325	        run: echo "No desktop-related changes since last release — skipping Desktop E2E gate"
326	
327	      - name: Summary
328	        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--')
329	        run: echo "All E2E gates passed — release is safe to merge."
330	
```
