1	name: Release Gate
2	
3	on:
4	  pull_request:
5	    branches: [main]
6	    types: [opened, synchronize, reopened]
7	
8	jobs:
9	  detect-changes:
10	    name: Detect Changes Since Last Release
11	    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
12	    outputs:
13	      desktop: ${{ steps.check.outputs.desktop || steps.release-check.outputs.desktop }}
14	      prev_tag: ${{ steps.check.outputs.prev_tag || steps.release-check.outputs.prev_tag }}
15	      is_release: ${{ steps.release-check.outputs.is_release }}
16	    steps:
17	      - name: Check if release PR
18	        id: release-check
19	        env:
20	          HEAD_REF: ${{ github.head_ref }}
21	        run: |
22	          if [[ "$HEAD_REF" == release-please--* ]]; then
23	            echo "is_release=true" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
24	          else
25	            echo "is_release=false" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
26	            echo "desktop=false" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
27	            echo "prev_tag=" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
28	            echo "Not a release PR — skipping change detection"
29	          fi
30	
31	      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
32	        if: steps.release-check.outputs.is_release == 'true'
33	        with:
34	          fetch-depth: 0
35	
36	      - name: Check for desktop-related changes since last release
37	        if: steps.release-check.outputs.is_release == 'true'
38	        id: check
39	        run: |
40	          # Find the latest non-staging release tag (cipher-box-v* format)
41	          PREV_TAG=$(git tag --list 'cipher-box-v*' --sort=-v:refname \
42	            | grep -v 'staging' \
43	            | head -1)
44	          # Fallback to legacy v* tags if no cipher-box-v* tags exist
45	          if [ -z "$PREV_TAG" ]; then
46	            PREV_TAG=$(git tag --list 'v*' --sort=-v:refname \
47	              | grep -v 'staging' \
48	              | grep -v '@' \
49	              | head -1)
50	          fi
51	
52	          if [ -z "$PREV_TAG" ]; then
53	            echo "No previous release tag found — assuming desktop changed"
54	            echo "desktop=true" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
55	            echo "prev_tag=" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
56	            exit 0
57	          fi
58	
59	          echo "Previous release tag: ${PREV_TAG}"
60	          echo "prev_tag=${PREV_TAG}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
61	          echo "Diffing ${PREV_TAG}..HEAD"
62	
63	          # Desktop-related path patterns (mirrored from desktop-e2e.yml)
64	          DESKTOP_PATTERNS=(
65	            'apps/desktop/src/'
66	            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/'
67	            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/vendor/'
68	            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/capabilities/'
69	            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/resources/'
70	            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/Cargo.toml'
71	            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/Cargo.lock'
72	            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/build.rs'
73	            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/rust-toolchain.toml'
74	            'apps/desktop/index.html'
75	            'apps/desktop/vite.config.'
76	            'apps/desktop/tsconfig'
77	            'packages/crypto/src/'
78	            'packages/crypto/tsconfig'
79	            '.github/workflows/desktop-e2e.yml'
80	          )
81	
82	          CHANGED_FILES=$(git diff --name-only "${PREV_TAG}..HEAD")
83	          DESKTOP_CHANGED=false
84	
85	          for pattern in "${DESKTOP_PATTERNS[@]}"; do
86	            if echo "$CHANGED_FILES" | grep -Fq "$pattern"; then
87	              echo "Desktop change detected matching pattern: ${pattern}"
88	              DESKTOP_CHANGED=true
89	              break
90	            fi
91	          done
92	
93	          echo "desktop=${DESKTOP_CHANGED}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
94	          echo "Desktop changes detected: ${DESKTOP_CHANGED}"
95	
96	  verify-e2e:
97	    name: Verify E2E Passed
98	    needs: detect-changes
99	    if: ${{ always() && !cancelled() }}
100	    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
101	    timeout-minutes: 40
102	    steps:
103	      - name: Fail if detect-changes did not complete (release PR)
104	        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--') && needs.detect-changes.result != 'success'
105	        run: |
106	          echo "::error::detect-changes must succeed before E2E verification."
107	          exit 1
108	
109	      - name: Skip (not a release PR)
110	        if: "!startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--')"
111	        run: echo "Not a release PR — E2E gate not applicable"
112	
113	      - name: Wait for Web E2E and verify it passed
114	        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--')
115	        env:
116	          GH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
117	        run: |
118	          # Get the latest commit on main (the base the release PR targets)
119	          MAIN_SHA=$(gh api repos/${{ github.repository }}/git/ref/heads/main --jq '.object.sha')
120	          echo "Latest main commit: ${MAIN_SHA}"
121	
122	          # Poll for the CI E2E orchestrator run to complete (it triggers on the same push)
123	          MAX_ATTEMPTS=30
124	          POLL_INTERVAL=30
125	          echo "Waiting for CI E2E run to complete (polling every ${POLL_INTERVAL}s, max ${MAX_ATTEMPTS} attempts)..."
126	
127	          for attempt in $(seq 1 $MAX_ATTEMPTS); do
128	            E2E_RUN=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
129	              --workflow=ci-e2e.yml --branch=main --limit=10 \
130	              --json headSha,conclusion,status,databaseId \
131	              --jq "[.[] | select(.headSha == \"${MAIN_SHA}\")][0] // empty")
132	            E2E_CONCLUSION=$(echo "$E2E_RUN" | jq -r '.conclusion // empty')
133	
134	            if [ -n "$E2E_CONCLUSION" ]; then
135	              echo "Web E2E completed with conclusion: ${E2E_CONCLUSION}"
136	              break
137	            fi
138	
139	            if [ "$attempt" -eq "$MAX_ATTEMPTS" ]; then
140	              echo "::error::Web E2E run did not complete within $(( MAX_ATTEMPTS * POLL_INTERVAL / 60 )) minutes for main (${MAIN_SHA})."
141	              exit 1
142	            fi
143	
144	            echo "Attempt ${attempt}/${MAX_ATTEMPTS}: E2E still running, waiting ${POLL_INTERVAL}s..."
145	            sleep $POLL_INTERVAL
146	          done
147	
148	          if [ "$E2E_CONCLUSION" != "success" ]; then
149	            echo "::error::CI E2E orchestrator did not pass on main (conclusion: ${E2E_CONCLUSION})"
150	            exit 1
151	          fi
152	
153	          # Verify the Web E2E job actually ran (not skipped due to no web changes)
154	          E2E_RUN_ID=$(echo "$E2E_RUN" | jq -r '.databaseId')
155	          WEB_E2E_RAN=$(gh api "repos/${{ github.repository }}/actions/runs/${E2E_RUN_ID}/jobs" \
156	            --jq '[.jobs[] | select(.name | contains("Web E2E")) | select(.conclusion == "success")] | length')
157	
158	          if [ "${WEB_E2E_RAN:-0}" -eq 0 ]; then
159	            echo "CI E2E passed but Web E2E was skipped (no web changes in latest push)."
160	            echo "Searching for most recent CI E2E run where Web E2E actually executed..."
161	
162	            RECENT_RUN_ID=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
163	              --workflow=ci-e2e.yml --branch=main --limit=20 \
164	              --json databaseId,conclusion,status \
165	              --jq '[.[] | select(.status == "completed" and .conclusion == "success")] | .[].databaseId' \
166	              | while read -r rid; do
167	                  WEB_COUNT=$(gh api "repos/${{ github.repository }}/actions/runs/${rid}/jobs" \
168	                    --jq '[.jobs[] | select(.name | contains("Web E2E")) | select(.conclusion == "success")] | length')
169	                  if [ "${WEB_COUNT:-0}" -gt 0 ]; then
170	                    echo "$rid"
171	                    break
172	                  fi
173	                done)
174	
175	            if [ -z "$RECENT_RUN_ID" ]; then
176	              echo "::error::No recent CI E2E run found where Web E2E tests actually executed."
177	              exit 1
178	            fi
179	            echo "Web E2E: passed (in CI E2E run ${RECENT_RUN_ID})"
180	          else
181	            echo "Web E2E: passed (at HEAD)"
182	          fi
183	
184	      - name: Verify Desktop E2E (if desktop changed)
185	        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--') && needs.detect-changes.outputs.desktop == 'true'
186	        env:
187	          GH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
188	          PREV_TAG: ${{ needs.detect-changes.outputs.prev_tag }}
189	        run: |
190	          MAIN_SHA=$(gh api repos/${{ github.repository }}/git/ref/heads/main --jq '.object.sha')
191	          echo "Desktop changes detected since ${PREV_TAG} — verifying Desktop E2E"
192	
193	          # Helper: check if a workflow run actually executed desktop-e2e jobs
194	          # (not skipped due to change detection). Returns 0 if tests ran, 1 if skipped, 2 on API error.
195	          run_executed_tests() {
196	            local run_id=$1
197	            local executed
198	            if ! executed=$(gh api "repos/${{ github.repository }}/actions/runs/${run_id}/jobs" \
199	              --jq '[.jobs[] | select(.name | startswith("Desktop E2E")) | select(.conclusion == "success")] | length'); then
200	              echo "::error::Failed to query jobs for run ${run_id} (API error or rate limit)"
201	              return 2
202	            fi
203	            [ "${executed:-0}" -gt 0 ]
204	          }
205	
206	          # First check if there's a desktop E2E run for HEAD (may still be running)
207	          HEAD_RUN=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
208	            --workflow=ci-e2e.yml --branch=main --limit=50 \
209	            --json headSha,conclusion,status,databaseId \
210	            --jq "[.[] | select(.headSha == \"${MAIN_SHA}\")][0]")
211	
212	          HEAD_STATUS=$(echo "$HEAD_RUN" | jq -r '.status // empty')
213	
214	          if [ -n "$HEAD_STATUS" ]; then
215	            HEAD_RUN_ID=$(echo "$HEAD_RUN" | jq -r '.databaseId')
216	            echo "Desktop E2E run exists for HEAD (run ${HEAD_RUN_ID}) — polling until complete..."
217	            MAX_ATTEMPTS=40
218	            POLL_INTERVAL=30
219	
220	            for attempt in $(seq 1 $MAX_ATTEMPTS); do
221	              CONCLUSION=$(gh run view "$HEAD_RUN_ID" --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
222	                --json conclusion \
223	                --jq '.conclusion // empty')
224	
225	              if [ -n "$CONCLUSION" ]; then
226	                if [ "$CONCLUSION" != "success" ]; then
227	                  echo "::error::Desktop E2E Tests did not pass on main (conclusion: ${CONCLUSION})"
228	                  exit 1
229	                fi
230	                # Verify the desktop-e2e jobs actually ran (not skipped)
231	                run_executed_tests "$HEAD_RUN_ID" && rc=0 || rc=$?
232	                if [ "$rc" -eq 2 ]; then
233	                  exit 1
234	                elif [ "$rc" -eq 0 ]; then
235	                  echo "Desktop E2E: passed (at HEAD, tests executed)"
236	                  exit 0
237	                else
238	                  echo "Desktop E2E run at HEAD succeeded but tests were skipped (no desktop changes detected by workflow)."
239	                  echo "Falling through to search for a run that actually executed tests..."
240	                  break
241	                fi
242	              fi
243	
244	              if [ "$attempt" -eq "$MAX_ATTEMPTS" ]; then
245	                echo "::error::Desktop E2E run did not complete within $(( MAX_ATTEMPTS * POLL_INTERVAL / 60 )) minutes."
246	                exit 1
247	              fi
248	
249	              echo "Attempt ${attempt}/${MAX_ATTEMPTS}: Desktop E2E still running, waiting ${POLL_INTERVAL}s..."
250	              sleep $POLL_INTERVAL
251	            done
252	          fi
253	
254	          # No desktop E2E run for HEAD (or HEAD run skipped tests) — find the
255	          # most recent run in the release range that actually executed tests
256	          echo "Searching for a Desktop E2E run that executed tests since ${PREV_TAG}..."
257	
258	          if [ -z "$PREV_TAG" ]; then
259	            echo "::error::No previous release tag and no Desktop E2E run for HEAD. Cannot verify desktop changes."
260	            exit 1
261	          fi
262	
263	          # Get commit SHAs in the release range
264	          RANGE_COMMITS=$(gh api "repos/${{ github.repository }}/compare/${PREV_TAG}...${MAIN_SHA}" \
265	            --jq '[.commits[].sha] | join("\n")' 2>/dev/null || echo "")
266	
267	          if [ -z "$RANGE_COMMITS" ]; then
268	            echo "::error::Could not determine commits in range ${PREV_TAG}..${MAIN_SHA}"
269	            exit 1
270	          fi
271	
272	          # Get recent completed desktop E2E runs (newest first) and find the
273	          # most recent one whose commit is within the release range AND whose
274	          # desktop-e2e jobs actually ran (not skipped)
275	          RUNS=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
276	            --workflow=ci-e2e.yml --branch=main --limit=50 \
277	            --json headSha,conclusion,status,databaseId \
278	            --jq '.[] | select(.status == "completed") | "\(.headSha) \(.conclusion) \(.databaseId)"')
279	
280	          LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA=""
281	          LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION=""
282	
283	          while IFS=" " read -r sha conclusion run_id; do
284	            if [ -z "$sha" ]; then
285	              continue
286	            fi
287	            if echo "$RANGE_COMMITS" | grep -Fq "$sha"; then
288	              if [ "$conclusion" != "success" ]; then
289	                # Found a failed run in range — report it immediately
290	                LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA="$sha"
291	                LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION="$conclusion"
292	                break
293	              fi
294	              # Success run — verify tests actually executed
295	              run_executed_tests "$run_id" && rc=0 || rc=$?
296	              if [ "$rc" -eq 2 ]; then
297	                exit 1
298	              elif [ "$rc" -eq 0 ]; then
299	                LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA="$sha"
300	                LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION="$conclusion"
301	                LATEST_IN_RANGE_RUN_ID="$run_id"
302	                break
303	              else
304	                echo "Skipping run ${run_id} at ${sha} — tests were skipped (no desktop changes detected by workflow)"
305	              fi
306	            fi
307	          done <<EOF
308	          $RUNS
309	          EOF
310	
311	          if [ -z "$LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA" ]; then
312	            echo "::error::No Desktop E2E run found (with tests actually executed) for any commit in ${PREV_TAG}..HEAD. Desktop code changed but was never verified."
313	            exit 1
314	          fi
315	
316	          if [ "$LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION" != "success" ]; then
317	            echo "::error::Most recent Desktop E2E run in range (at ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA}) did not succeed (conclusion: ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_CONCLUSION})."
318	            exit 1
319	          fi
320	
321	          echo "Desktop E2E: passed (run ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_RUN_ID} at ${LATEST_IN_RANGE_SHA} with tests executed)"
322	
323	      - name: Skip Desktop E2E (no desktop changes)
324	        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--') && needs.detect-changes.outputs.desktop != 'true'
325	        run: echo "No desktop-related changes since last release — skipping Desktop E2E gate"
326	
327	      - name: Summary
328	        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--')
329	        run: echo "All E2E gates passed — release is safe to merge."
330	
```

{

Now unified in one place.\n\n## Web path patterns\n`apps/web/`, `apps/api/`, `packages/`, `tests/web-e2e/`, `tools/mock-ipns-routing/`, `.github/workflows/e2e.yml`, `.github/workflows/ci-e2e.yml`\n\n## Desktop path patterns\n(Moved from `desktop-e2e.yml`, unchanged)\n\n## Test plan\n- [ ] Push to main touching only `tests/web-e2e/` → web E2E runs, desktop E2E skips\n- [ ] Push to main touching only `apps/desktop/` → desktop E2E runs, web E2E skips\n- [ ] Push to main touching `packages/crypto/` → both run (it's in both path lists)\n- [ ] Push to main touching only `.planning/` → both skip, retrigger still runs\n- [ ] `workflow_dispatch` on `e2e.yml` or `desktop-e2e.yml` → runs unconditionally\n- [ ] Release gate on release-please PR verifies web E2E job conclusion, not just orchestrator success\n- [ ] Retrigger job finds and re-runs release gate after E2E completes\n\n🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)\nEOF\n)\"",

**Line 219 cannot achieve its stated goal—re-running a stale `release-gate.yml` run will not re-evaluate against current main.**

When `gh run rerun` re-executes a workflow triggered by `pull_request`, it preserves the original `GITHUB_SHA` and `GITHUB_REF`. Inside `release-gate.yml`, line 82 diffs `${PREV_TAG}..HEAD`, where `HEAD` remains the original PR's commit SHA from the first run, not main's current HEAD. This means any desktop changes merged to main after the first run will remain invisible to `detect-changes`, causing the re-run gate to pass incorrectly. The comment's suggestion to use a dispatchable/reusable entrypoint or resolve main dynamically inside `release-gate.yml` is necessary to actually verify against current main.

<details>
<summary>🤖 Prompt for AI Agents</summary>

```
Verify each finding against the current code and only fix it if needed.

In @.github/workflows/e2e.yml around lines 208 - 220, The current step uses gh
run rerun which preserves the original GITHUB_SHA/GITHUB_REF so
release-gate.yml's detect-changes (the PREV_TAG..HEAD diff) still compares
against the stale PR HEAD; replace the gh run rerun invocation with either (A)
dispatching the release-gate workflow on main (use gh workflow run /
workflow_dispatch API to run release-gate.yml with --ref main or via the
workflow's dispatch endpoint) so the workflow executes with main's current HEAD,
or (B) change release-gate.yml's detect-changes logic to resolve main
dynamically (git fetch origin main and diff PREV_TAG..origin/main instead of
PREV_TAG..HEAD) so reruns correctly compare against current main; refer to the
gh run rerun call, release-gate.yml, and the detect-changes / PREV_TAG..HEAD
comparison to implement one of these fixes.
```

</details>

> AGENT

Valid concern. On a re-run, `HEAD` would be the stale release-please PR merge commit, not current main. The `verify-e2e` steps use `gh api` to resolve main dynamically (so web E2E is fine), but the desktop `detect-changes` diff uses `HEAD`. Fix: diff against `origin/main` instead. `actions/checkout` with `fetch-depth: 0` fetches the full repo including current `origin/main`.

> DEVELOPER

the pr description mentions that a push to main touching only apps/desktop would cause the desktop e2e to run. does this include any changes to upstream rust crates?

> AGENT

Yes — the desktop patterns include:

- `crates/**` — all workspace Rust crates
- `Cargo.toml` — root workspace manifest
- `Cargo.lock` — root lockfile (dependency version changes)

So any change to a Rust crate in `crates/` would trigger desktop E2E. You can see the full list in `ci-e2e.yml` lines 32-51.

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/.github/workflows/release-gate.yml",
  "old_string": "          # When ci-e2e.yml calls e2e.yml via workflow_call, the job name is\n          # composed as \"Web E2E / Web E2E Tests\" — contains(\"Web E2E\") matches both.\n          WEB_E2E_RAN=$(gh api \"repos/${{ github.repository }}/actions/runs/${E2E_RUN_ID}/jobs\" \\\n            --jq '[.jobs[] | select(.name | contains(\"Web E2E\")) | select(.conclusion == \"success\")] | length')\n\n          if [ \"${WEB_E2E_RAN:-0}\" -eq 0 ]; then\n            echo \"CI E2E passed but Web E2E was skipped (no web changes in latest push).\"\n            echo \"Searching for most recent CI E2E run where Web E2E actually executed...\"\n\n            RECENT_RUN_ID=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \\\n              --workflow=ci-e2e.yml --branch=main --limit=20 \\\n              --json databaseId,conclusion,status \\\n              --jq '[.[] | select(.status == \"completed\" and .conclusion == \"success\")] | .[].databaseId' \\\n              | while read -r rid; do\n                  WEB_COUNT=$(gh api \"repos/${{ github.repository }}/actions/runs/${rid}/jobs\" \\\n                    --jq '[.jobs[] | select(.name | contains(\"Web E2E\")) | select(.conclusion == \"success\")] | length')\n                  if [ \"${WEB_COUNT:-0}\" -gt 0 ]; then\n                    echo \"$rid\"\n                    break\n                  fi\n                done)",
  "new_string": "          # When ci-e2e.yml calls e2e.yml via workflow_call, the job name is\n          # composed as \"Web E2E / Web E2E Tests\" — contains(\"Web E2E\") matches both.\n          if ! WEB_E2E_RAN=$(gh api \"repos/${{ github.repository }}/actions/runs/${E2E_RUN_ID}/jobs\" \\\n            --jq '[.jobs[] | select(.name | contains(\"Web E2E\")) | select(.conclusion == \"success\")] | length'); then\n            echo \"::error::Failed to query jobs for CI E2E run ${E2E_RUN_ID} (API error)\"\n            exit 1\n          fi\n\n          if [ \"${WEB_E2E_RAN:-0}\" -eq 0 ]; then\n            echo \"CI E2E passed but Web E2E was skipped (no web changes in latest push).\"\n            echo \"Searching for most recent CI E2E run where Web E2E actually executed...\"\n\n            RECENT_RUN_ID=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \\\n              --workflow=ci-e2e.yml --branch=main --limit=20 \\\n              --json databaseId,conclusion,status \\\n              --jq '[.[] | select(.status == \"completed\" and .conclusion == \"success\")] | .[].databaseId' \\\n              | while read -r rid; do\n                  if ! WEB_COUNT=$(gh api \"repos/${{ github.repository }}/actions/runs/${rid}/jobs\" \\\n                    --jq '[.jobs[] | select(.name | contains(\"Web E2E\")) | select(.conclusion == \"success\")] | length'); then\n                    echo \"::warning::Failed to query jobs for run ${rid}, skipping\" >&2\n                    continue\n                  fi\n                  if [ \"${WEB_COUNT:-0}\" -gt 0 ]; then\n                    echo \"$rid\"\n                    break\n                  fi\n                done)"
}

> AGENT

No global test timeout is set in the Playwright config — that's fine, individual tests have their own timeouts. The only relevant timeout is the job-level `timeout-minutes: 20` we just set.

But the web server `timeout: 120000` (2 minutes each for API and web dev server) could be slow on CI. Those are startup timeouts though, not runtime.

Let me check the CI workflow more carefully — in CI, the workflow pre-builds everything and starts servers manually. The `webServer` config has `reuseExistingServer: !process.env.CI`, meaning in CI it does NOT reuse and would try to start servers again.

> AGENT

The CI workflow builds everything but then runs `pnpm --filter @cipherbox/web-e2e test` which invokes Playwright. Since `CI=true` and `reuseExistingServer: !process.env.CI` evaluates to `false`, Playwright will start its own API and web servers via the `webServer` config. That's where time is going — Playwright is starting `pnpm --filter @cipherbox/api dev` and `pnpm --filter @cipherbox/web dev` from scratch in CI, which includes compilation time.

But that's the existing design and not what's causing a 10-minute hard cutoff. The 20-minute job timeout should be sufficient now. There's no other 10-minute timeout in the config — the previous 15-minute limit was the only hard ceiling.
